## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- ogx-k8s-operator is a Kubernetes component.
- managing 2 CRD(s)\: LlamaStackDistribution, OGXServer.
- deploying 2 workload(s) (deployment, ogx-k8s-operator-controller-manager) with 1 container(s).
- watching 10 resource type(s).
- exposing 2 service(s) on ports\: 443, 8443.
- with 1 webhook(s) (1 validating).
- protected by 1 network policy/policies.
- with 2 PodDisruptionBudget(s).
- with 1 HorizontalPodAutoscaler(s).
- defining 23 API spec type(s) across 53 struct(s) with 187 total fields.
- with 78 operator config constants.
- reporting 11 status condition types.
- with 1 ingress route(s).
- integrates with management relationship via labels/annotations.
- kustomize overlays\: config/certmanager, config/crd, config/default, config/manager, config/manifests, config/overlays/cert-manager, config/overlays/openshift, config/prometheus, config/rbac, config/scorecard, config/webhook.
